thrust runs straight and penetrates, speech or cloth is tightly made, and a joint or vessel matches its proper seat. Joined straps, sound weaving, and a knitted bone extend this exactness into assembly and repair, while forceful footing tests whether the construction holds.","ayah_refs":["69:1","69:2","69:3","69:9","69:14","69:12"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"C","name":"Blast, roar, and receptive ear","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A forceful emission becomes signal, and a listening organ must receive and register it.","active_motifs":"إرسال الريح في الشيء (`quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`); قرن النفخ (`quranic:root_000891:B004/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); الخَوَاة صوتا (`quranic:root_000450:B007/m01`); ارتفاع الصوت والجلبة (`quranic:root_001664:B005/m01`); الأذن الجارحة وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); العلم والإعلام بالنداء (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m01`); طغي القوم صوتهم (`quranic:root_000936:B008/m01`); شقشقة البعير والخطيب (`quranic:root_000807:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ)","synthesis":"Blowing into the horn turns breath into a public acoustic event. Roar, clamor, collective voice, and the camel's or orator's protruding throat-sound converge on an ear that is both a physical receiver and an act of obedient attention; the call becomes knowledge when announced and heard. The `ط غ و` voice motif has no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:13","69:6","69:7","69:12","69:16"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Vertical forms are hollowed, split, and leveled","parent_semantic_invariant":"Tall bodies, palms, buildings, mountains, and sky lose their interior support and vertical distinction.","parent_surprising_reach":"Ruin is rendered through carpentry and anatomy: empty belly, uprooted trunk, loosened wall, torn skin, buried well, and sand ridge all undergo related structural failure.","key":"B","name":"Wall, roof, skin, and vessel rupture","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A containing surface loosens, tears, or opens until what it shelters can no longer be held.","active_motifs":"انصداع الشيء وانفتاحه (`quranic:root_000807:B001/m01`); النصف والشق المقابل (`quranic:root_000807:B002/m01`); قطعة منشقة وثوب (`quranic:root_000807:B006/m01`); استرخاء وضعف في الشيء (`quranic:root_001688:B001/m01`); شق وخرق في أديم أو ثوب أو سقاء (`quranic:root_001688:B002/m01`); إيهاء اليد بكسر أو إصابة (`quranic:root_001688:B004/m01`); السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); قوام الأمر والعز إذا ثبت أو ثل (`quranic:root_001000:B006/m01`); الدق والهدم حتى التسوية (`quranic:root_000482:B001/m01`); إهالة التراب والدفن (`quranic:root_000482:B003/m01`); الأَرَضَة آكلة الخشب (`quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`); فساد القرحة بالمدة (`quranic:root_000025:B011/m01`); امتلاء الجرح بالمدة (`quranic:root_001664:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `و ه ي`: 69:16 (وَاهِيَةٌ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ)","synthesis":"Crack, split side, detached piece, loose wall, unpatchable skin, and broken hand describe one failure of enclosure. Roof and sovereignty can both be 'thrown down'; demolition and burial fill the breach from above, while worm-eaten wood, suppurating tissue, and a pus-filled wound show slower versions of the same loss of integrity.","ayah_refs":["69:16","69:17","69:14","69:12"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.","parent_surprising_reach":"The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.","key":"B","name":"Catch basin, wellhead, and receiving vessel","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water is arrested in a depression, drawn or poured, and handed into a durable receptacle.","active_motifs":"موضع يمسك الماء (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); مجتمع الماء في هبطة أو حول حصن (`quranic:root_000281:B003/m01`); الجِيأة مجتمع الماء (`quranic:root_000282:B002/m01`); وقائع الماء ومناقعه (`quranic:root_001675:B008/m01`); خشب البئر وموضع الساقي (`quranic:root_001000:B004/m01`); ماء رَبَب كثير (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); تلقي الخارج إلى اليد (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`); سقي على أفواه الإبل (`quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`); إيصال الماء بالسقي والصب (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); ضم الشيء في وعاء (`quranic:root_001664:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ)","synthesis":"Pool, fortress-side cistern, pond, timbered wellhead, abundant water, receiving assistant, and mouth-level watering form a complete collection system. Pouring moves the resource into a vessel, and the vessel's defining operation is to gather and keep what would otherwise continue downstream.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:9","69:15","69:17","69:11","69:12"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept","parent_semantic_invariant":"Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.","parent_surprising_reach":"The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.","key":"B","name":"Listening, permission, and inward retention","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"The body opens an authorized point of reception and stores what enters as understood meaning.","active_motifs":"الأذن الجارحة وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); العلم والإعلام بالنداء (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m01`); الإذن والترخيص بالأمر (`quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`); ضم المعنى في القلب (`quranic:root_001664:B001/m01`); ضم الشيء في وعاء (`quranic:root_001664:B002/m01`); انعدام المنصرف أو البد (`quranic:root_001664:B006/m01`); القائم على اليتيم (`quranic:root_001664:B007/m01`); استحضار الشيء بعد النسيان أو مع الحفظ (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ)","synthesis":"The ear is both organ and attentive acceptance; announcement makes the content known, and permission governs entry. The heart then contains meaning as a vessel contains provisions, treats it as indispensable, and places it under care so that recollection can reactivate it.","ayah_refs":["69:12","69:3"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept","parent_semantic_invariant":"Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.","parent_surprising_reach":"The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.","key":"C","name":"Deed, covenant, and protected remembrance","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A socially binding memory is held in a document, pledge, or protected charge.","active_motifs":"ذكر الحق صك ووثيقة حق (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); حقيقة يلزم حفظها (`quranic:root_000347:B007/m01`); ربابة تجمع القداح (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); ضمان الشيء والتكفل به (`quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`); تحمّل الأمانة والوزر (`quranic:root_000357:B003/m01`); الحمالة والكفالة (`quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`); مِلاك الأمر وعِماده (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); توقيع الكتاب وأثره (`quranic:root_001675:B011/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"A deed of right, reminder, covenant, quiver-like repository, guarantee, carried trust, and written signature all preserve an obligation beyond the moment of speech. The affair has a structural mainstay, and failure is not mere forgetting but a breach in custody.","ayah_refs":["69:12","69:1","69:2","69:3","69:10","69:17","69:9","69:11","69:14","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: A message is sent, heard, and kept","parent_semantic_invariant":"Communication is a custody chain in which a charge is dispatched, carried, received, retained, and made recallable.","parent_surprising_reach":"The communication chain is materialized as herd after herd, flowing provision, a carried burden, an inward vessel, a legal deed, and an ear that acts as a gate of permission.","key":"D","name":"Spoken name, reputation, and public recurrence","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Repeated speech makes a person or event present in communal memory as name, fame, praise, or blame.","active_motifs":"جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); رياء الناس (`quranic:root_000531:B005/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); استقامة واعتدال واستواء (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); ردع يقرع السامع (`quranic:root_001219:B006/m01`); الجَعْلة اسم مكان (`quranic:root_000248:B011/m01`); العلم بموضع أو يوم (`quranic:root_000450:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Tongued mention and carried message give a name public reach; favorable renown can rise, while place-name and named day stabilize reference. Performance for spectators and rebuke expose distorted social uses of visibility, and straight speech supplies the norm by which praise, blame, and reputation are heard.","ayah_refs":["69:12","69:16","69:7","69:8","69:10","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Sovereignty rests on raised supports","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule is sustained by a hierarchy of throne, upper position, structural mainstay, delegated care, and communal covenant.","parent_surprising_reach":"Royal elevation is tested through architecture and husbandry: a throne may be broken, a roof propped, a well timbered, a flock led, and a polity maintained by water and covenant.","key":"D","name":"Alliance, gathered people, and binding pledge","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Groups become a polity through assembly, recognized divisions, and a covenant that holds them together.","active_motifs":"ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); ربابة تجمع القداح (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); رَبْرَب قطيع (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); جماعة الناس والرجال (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); ضمان الشيء والتكفل به (`quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`); جماعة يقبل بعضها على بعض (`quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`); كثرة الرجال واجتماعهم (`quranic:root_001664:B008/m01`); كثرة كالجبل (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ)","synthesis":"Large gatherings, lots held in one container, herds, councils, tribes, and crowds are not yet a polity until a pledge and guarantor bind their divisions. Place of assembly and sheer multitude provide the social material; covenant supplies the relation that makes it durable.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:17","69:7","69:9","69:12","69:14"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"A","name":"Mating, conception, and approaching birth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Male and female animals meet, conception becomes visible, and a carried young approaches emergence.","active_motifs":"ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); ضراب الفحل وإنزاؤه (`quranic:root_001219:B003/m01`); ظهور حمل الناقة أو الشاة (`quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`); تحصن الفرس وصيرورته فحلا (`quranic:root_000609:B018/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل من الضأن (`quranic:root_000357:B009/m01`); ماء الفحل في الرحم (`quranic:root_001458:B004/m01`); اشتهاء الأنثى للفحل (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); فرخ النعام (`quranic:root_000248:B010/m01`); الحامل تدنو ولادتها (`quranic:root_000548:B005/m01`); رفع الحمار رأسه عند الحمل (`quranic:root_001000:B009/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); الطغيا الصغير من بقر الوحش (`quranic:root_000936:B009/m01`); تطاول الفحل على الشول (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); إلقاء الثقل في المخالطة (`quranic:root_000482:B009/m01`); المواقعة في الجماع (`quranic:root_001675:B014/m01`); ترية الحيض (`quranic:root_000531:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"Breeding readiness, mounting, deposited seed, visible pregnancy, inward burden, swelling, menstrual sign, and the nearness of birth form a continuous generational scene. A stallion rising over mares and bodily weight in intercourse make coupling explicit; young camel, lamb, ostrich chick, and wild-cattle calf mark successive products. The `ط غ و` calf motif has no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:1","69:2","69:3","69:4","69:7","69:8","69:6","69:11","69:14","69:17","69:12","69:10","69:13","69:16","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"D","name":"Tack, restraint, shelter, and mounted frame","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Equipment connects animal body to load, rider, shelter, and restraint.","active_motifs":"آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); عريش المرأة على البعير (`quranic:root_001000:B005/m01`); منع الغنم من الرعي (`quranic:root_001000:B010/m01`); ركوب الدابة واعتلاؤها (`quranic:root_001000:B011/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); التحامل والمشقة (`quranic:root_000357:B007/m01`); قبول الشيء برضا (`quranic:root_001198:B004/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`); إقبال العضو أو العلامة إلى جهة (`quranic:root_001198:B011/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); فوق السهم موضع الوتر (`quranic:root_001188:B009/m01`); مواضع علو أو شق في الجسد والآلة (`quranic:root_001188:B013/m01`); جماعة النخل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000891:B005/m01`); خرقة إنزال القدر (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ)","synthesis":"Standing hardware, diseased or constrained legs, livestock canopy, litter, grazing barrier, saddle or mount, load-bearing straps, udder cover, bridle parts, handle, notched fittings, and palm-shaped forelock create a detailed equipment scene. Even the heat-protective cloth belongs to this material practice of handling bodies and burdens.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:17","69:11","69:14","69:9","69:10","69:13","69:12"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"B","name":"Palm stand, shoots, and represented grove","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Palms appear as living tree, grouped grove, short shoot, decorative form, and named place.","active_motifs":"شجرة النخل (`quranic:root_001483:B001/m01`); حلي على صورة النخل (`quranic:root_001483:B005/m01`); أعلام المواضع والأشخاص (`quranic:root_001483:B006/m01`); جماعة النخل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000891:B005/m01`); النخل الصغار أو القصار (`quranic:root_000248:B006/m01`); السقوط والانهدام (`quranic:root_000450:B002/m01`); ربة نبات (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ن خ ل`: 69:7 (نَخْلٍ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ)","synthesis":"Tree and grove provide the direct image, but the palm is also reproduced as ornament, forelock-like form, short planting, and toponym. Uprooting reverses this multiplication, and the persistent green plant preserves the living form against the scene of collapse.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:13","69:12","69:10","69:17"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"D","name":"Garden produce, insects, and damaged wood","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A cultivated plot contains edible growth alongside small creatures that inhabit or damage it.","active_motifs":"قرع اليقطين (`quranic:root_001219:B009/m01`); دويبة الجعلان (`quranic:root_000248:B008/m01`); دويبة الصرصر (`quranic:root_000857:B001/m01`); الأَرَضَة آكلة الخشب (`quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ)","synthesis":"Gourd, dung beetle, small chirping creature, and wood-eating termite create a compact garden ecology. The plant is not isolated produce: it shares soil and timber with organisms that consume, inhabit, or acoustically mark the plot.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:12","69:6","69:14"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Community is sustained by kin care and exchange","parent_semantic_invariant":"Groups endure through lineage, guardianship, gift, valuation, labor, and the circulation of benefit.","parent_surprising_reach":"The social body includes outsider, foundling, stepchild, elder, clan, guarantor, wage, tribute, market, and returning favor.","key":"A","name":"Clan, tribe, crowd, and recognized elder","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Individuals are placed inside lineage and assembled groups with visible leaders.","active_motifs":"جماعة الناس والرجال (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); جماعة يقبل بعضها على بعض (`quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`); ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); رَبْرَب قطيع (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); كثرة كالجبل (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`); سادات كالجِبال (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); كثرة الرجال واجتماعهم (`quranic:root_001664:B008/m01`); الحميل المحمول في النسب (`quranic:root_000357:B005/m01`); آخر الولد عند كبر الأبوين (`quranic:root_000985:B007/m01`); عود مسن فيه بقايا قوة (`quranic:root_001058:B008/m01`); الخليق بالخير كالأرض الأريضة (`quranic:root_000025:B003/m01`); ابن الأرض الغريب (`quranic:root_000025:B004/m01`); أهل البيت من بني الأعمام (`quranic:root_000537:B007/m01`); عاد وأعلام منسوبة إليها (`quranic:root_001058:B012/m01`); نسبة الهالكي إلى الحداد (`quranic:root_001596:B007/m01`); انصداع الجماعة بالخلاف (`quranic:root_000807:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
